Lemma 9

(Lemma 17 from
54
)
.

Let
\|\theta^{t+1}_{i}\|\leq b
for all
i\in\mathcal{G}.
Let each
f_{i}
be
L
-smooth. Then, for the iterates generated by
Byz-Clip21-SGD2M
the quantity
\widetilde{P}^{t}\coloneqq\frac{1}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\|v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\|^{2}
decreases as

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}^{t+1}\leq(1-\beta)\widetilde{P}^{t}+\frac{3L^{2}}{\beta}R^{t}+\beta^{2}b^{2}+\frac{2}{G}\beta(1-\beta)\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle,

(63)

where
R^{t}\coloneqq\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\|^{2}
and
\theta^{t}_{i}=\nabla f_{i}(x^{t},\xi^{t}_{i})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}).

Lemma 10

(Lemma 18 from
54
)
.

Let
\|\theta^{t+1}\|\leq\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{G}}
. Let each
f_{i}
be
L
-smooth. Then, for the iterates generated by
Byz-Clip21-SGD2M
the quantity
\widetilde{P}^{t}\coloneqq\|\overline{v}^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t})\|^{2}
decreases as

\displaystyle\widetilde{P}^{t+1}\leq(1-\beta)\widetilde{P}^{t}+\frac{3L^{2}}{\beta}R^{t}+\beta^{2}\frac{c^{2}}{G}+2\beta(1-\beta)\langle\overline{v}^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}\rangle,

(64)

where
R^{t}\coloneqq\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\|^{2}
and
\theta^{t}=\frac{1}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\nabla f_{i}(x^{t},\xi^{t}_{i})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}).

Lemma 11

(Lemma 19 from
54
)
.

Let
\|\theta^{t}_{i}\|\leq b
for all
i\in\mathcal{G},
each
f_{i}
be
L
-smooth, and
\|v_{i}^{t}-g_{i}^{t-1}\|\leq B_{\rm init}
for all
i\in\mathcal{G}
and some
B_{\rm init}>\tau,
and
\hat{\beta}\leq\frac{1}{2\eta}
. Then for the iterates generated by
Byz-Clip21-SGD2M
we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|g_{i}^{t}-v_{i}^{t}\|^{2}&\leq(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)\|g_{i}^{t-1}-v_{i}^{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{4\beta^{2}}{\hat{\beta}\eta}\|v_{i}^{t-1}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t-1})\|^{2}+\frac{4\beta^{2}L^{2}}{\hat{\beta}\eta}R^{t-1}+\beta^{2}b^{2}\\
&\qquad+\;2(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\beta\langle(g_{i}^{t-1}-v_{i}^{t-1})+\beta(v_{i}^{t-1}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t-1})),\theta^{t}_{i}\rangle\\
&\qquad+\;2(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\beta^{2}\langle\nabla f_{i}(x^{t-1})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}),\theta^{t}_{i}\rangle,\end{aligned}

(65)

where
R^{t}\coloneqq\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\|^{2}
and
\eta\coloneqq\frac{\tau}{B_{\rm init}}\in(0,1).
Moreover, averaging the inequalities over
i\in\mathcal{G}
, we get

\displaystyle\widetilde{V}^{t}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)\widetilde{V}^{t-1}+\frac{4\beta^{2}}{\hat{\beta}\eta}\widetilde{P}^{t-1}+\frac{4\beta^{2}L^{2}}{\hat{\beta}\eta}R^{t-1}+\beta^{2}b^{2}

(66)

\displaystyle\qquad+\;\frac{2}{G}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\beta\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle(g_{i}^{t-1}-v_{i}^{t-1})+\beta(v_{i}^{t-1}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t-1}))+\beta(\nabla f_{i}(x^{t-1})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})),\theta^{t}_{i}\rangle,

where
\widetilde{V}^{t}\coloneqq\frac{1}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\|g_{i}^{t}-v_{i}^{t}\|^{2}
and
\widetilde{P}^{t}\coloneqq\frac{1}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\|v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\|^{2}.

Let us define constants that we will use in the proof of the main theorem.

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} a&\coloneqq\left(\sqrt{2}+2\sqrt{3\log\frac{8(T+1)}{\alpha}}\right)\sqrt{d}\sigma_{\omega}\sqrt{\frac{T}{G}},\\
\hat{a}&\coloneqq\left(\sqrt{2}+2\sqrt{3\log\frac{8G(T+1)}{\alpha}}\right)\sqrt{d}\sigma_{\omega}\sqrt{T},\\
b^{2}&\coloneqq 2\sigma^{2}\log\left(\frac{16(T+1)G}{\alpha}\right),\\
\hat{c}^{2}&\coloneqq\left(\sqrt{2}+2\sqrt{3\log\frac{8(T+1)}{\alpha}}\right)^{2}\sigma^{2},\end{aligned}

(67)

Appendix D
Proof of Theorem
5.1

Theorem D.1

(Full statement of
Theorem

5.1
)
.

Let Assumptions
3.1
and
3.2
, and define
B_{\rm init}\coloneqq\max\{3\tau,\max_{i}\{\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{0})\|\}+b\}
. Let the failure probability
\alpha
be such that
\alpha\in(0,1)
, and constants
a,\hat{a},b,
and
c
be defined as in (
67
), and
\Delta\geq\Phi^{0}
for
\Phi^{0}
defined in (
9
). Consider the run of
Byz-Clip21-SGD2M
(
Algorithm

1
) for
T
iterations with DP noise variance
\sigma_{\omega}
.
Assume the following inequalities hold

1.

step-size restrictions:

(a)

\gamma\leq\frac{1}{24L}
;

(b)

12L\gamma=\beta
;

(c)

\frac{5}{6}-\frac{32L^{2}\beta^{2}}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\gamma^{2}-\frac{96L^{2}}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\gamma^{2}\geq 0.

(68)

2.

momentum restrictions:

(a)

\beta\leq\min\left\{\frac{\hat{\beta}\tau}{54\sqrt{L\Delta}},1\right\};

(b)

\beta\leq\min\left\{\frac{2\hat{\beta}\tau}{39(B_{\rm init}-\tau)},1\right\};

(c)

\beta\leq\min\left\{\frac{2\hat{\beta}\tau}{69b},1\right\};

(d)

\beta\leq\min\left\{\frac{2}{41\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}T},1\right\}
;

(e)

\hat{\beta}\in\min\left\{\frac{\sqrt{L\Delta}}{a},\frac{\sqrt{L\Delta}}{4\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}},1\right\};

(f)

and momentum restrictions defined in (
71
), (
72
), (
D
), (
D
), (
75
), (
77
), (
76
), and (
78
);

Then, with probability
1-\alpha
, we bound
\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\|\nabla f(x^{t})\|^{2}
with

\displaystyle\sqrt{L\Delta}(\sqrt{L\Delta}+B_{\rm init}+\sigma)\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d}\sigma_{\omega}}{\sqrt{GT}\tau}+\frac{d^{1/3}\sigma_{\omega}^{2/3}}{(TG)^{1/3}\tau^{2/3}}+\frac{\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}\sqrt{d}\sigma_{\omega}}{\sqrt{T}\tau}+\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}\right),

where
\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}
hides constant and logarithmic factors and higher order terms decreasing in
T
. Let additionally
3.3a
hold, then

\sqrt{L\Delta}(\sqrt{L\Delta}+B_{\rm init}+\sigma)=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left((1+B_{\zeta})LF^{0}+\zeta^{2}+\sigma((1+\sqrt{B_{\zeta}})\sqrt{LF^{0}}+\zeta)\right).

Proof.

For convenience, we define
\nabla f_{i}(x^{-1},\xi^{-1}_{i})=v_{i}^{-1}=g_{i}^{-1}=0,\Phi^{-1}=\Phi^{0}
. Next, let us define an event
E^{t}
for each
t\in\{0,\dots,T\}
such that the following inequalities hold for all
k\in\{0,\dots,t\}

1.

\|v_{i}^{k}-g_{i}^{k-1}\|\leq B_{\rm init}
for
i\in\mathcal{I}_{k};

2.

\|\overline{m}^{t}\|\leq\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\hat{\beta}a+\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+3\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T
,

3.

\|\overline{g}^{t}\|\leq\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T;

4.

\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-v_{i}^{t}\|\leq\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T
for all
i\in\mathcal{G};

5.

\|\theta^{k}_{i}\|\leq b
for all
i\in\mathcal{G}
and
\|\theta^{k}\|\leq\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{G}};

6.

\left\|\frac{1}{G}\sum_{l=1}^{k+1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\omega_{i}^{l}\right\|\leq a
and
\left\|\sum_{l=1}^{k+1}\omega_{i}^{l}\right\|\leq\hat{a}
for all
i\in\mathcal{G}
;

7.

\Phi^{k}\leq 2\Delta
;

8.

\displaystyle\frac{7}{8}\Delta

\displaystyle\geq\frac{4\gamma\beta}{n\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\eta)^{2}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle(g_{i}^{l}-v_{i}^{l})+\beta(v_{i}^{l}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{l}))+\beta(\nabla f_{i}(x^{l})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{l+1})),\theta^{t}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{n\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle v_{i}^{l}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{l}),\theta^{l+1}_{i}\rangle+4\gamma(1-\beta)\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\langle v^{l}-\nabla f(x^{l}),\theta^{l+1}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{15\gamma\beta^{2}}{n\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\nabla f_{i}(x^{l})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{l+1}),\theta^{l+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+4\gamma(1-\beta)\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\langle\nabla f(x^{l})-\nabla f(x^{l+1}),\theta^{l+1}\rangle.

Then, we will derive the result by induction, i.e., using the induction w.r.t.
t
, we will show that
\mathbb{P}(E^{t})\geq 1-\frac{\alpha(t+1)}{T+1}
for all
t\in\{0,\dots,T-1\}
.

Before moving on to the proof’s induction part, we need to establish several useful bounds. Denote the events
\Theta^{t}_{i},\Theta^{t}
and
N^{t+1}
as

\displaystyle\Theta^{t}_{i}\coloneqq\{\|\theta^{t}_{i}\|\geq b\},\quad\Theta^{t}\coloneqq\left\{\|\theta^{t}\|\geq\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{G}}\right\},\quad N^{t+1}\coloneqq\left\{\left\|\frac{1}{G}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\omega_{i}^{l}\right\|\geq a\right\},\quad\text{and}

\displaystyle N_{i}^{t+1}=\left\{\left\|\sum_{l=1}^{t}\omega_{i}^{l}\right\|\geq\hat{a}\right\}

(69)

respectively. From
3.2
we have

\mathbb{P}(\Theta^{t}_{i})\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)=\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)G}

where the last equality is by definition of
b^{2}
. Therefore,
\mathbb{P}(\overline{\Theta}^{t}_{i})\geq 1-\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)G}.

Besides, notice that the constant
\hat{c}
in (
67
) can be viewed as

\hat{c}^{2}=(\sqrt{2}+2b_{3})\sigma^{2}\quad\text{where}\quad b_{3}^{2}=3\log\frac{8(T+1)}{\alpha}.

Now, we can use
Lemma

2
to bound
\mathbb{P}(\Theta^{t}).
Since all
\theta^{t}_{i}
are independent
\sigma
-sub-Gaussian random vectors, then we have

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\theta^{t}_{i}\right\|\geq\hat{c}\sqrt{G}\right)=\mathbb{P}\left(\|\theta^{t}\|\geq\frac{\hat{c}}{\sqrt{G}}\right)\leq\exp(-b_{3}^{2}/3)=\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}.

We also use
Lemma

2
to bound
\mathbb{P}(N^{t})
and
\mathbb{P}(N_{i}^{t})
. Indeed, since all
\omega_{i}^{l}
are independent Gaussian random vectors, then we have

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{l=1}^{t}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\omega_{i}^{l}\right\|\geq(\sqrt{2}+2b_{2})\sqrt{\sum_{l=1}^{t}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\sigma_{\omega}^{2}d}\right)\leq\exp(-\nicefrac{{b_{2}^{2}}}{{3}})=\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}.

with
b_{2}^{2}=3\log\left(\frac{8(T+1)}{\alpha}\right).

This implies that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{G}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\omega_{i}^{l}\right\|\geq a\right)\leq\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}

due to the choice of
a
from (
67
):

a=(\sqrt{2}+2b_{2})\sigma_{\omega}\sqrt{d}\sqrt{\frac{T}{G}},\quad\text{where}\quad b_{2}^{2}=3\log\frac{8(T+1)}{\alpha}.

Note that with this choice of
a
we have that the above is true for any
t\in\{1,\ldots,T\}
, i.e.,
\mathbb{P}(N^{t})\geq 1-\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}
for all
t\in\{1,\ldots,T\}.
Similarly, we derive

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{l=1}^{t}\omega_{i}^{l}\right\|\geq(\sqrt{2}+2b_{4})\sqrt{\sum_{l=1}^{t}\sigma^{2}_{\omega}d}\right)\leq\exp(-\nicefrac{{b_{4}^{2}}}{{3}})\leq\frac{\alpha}{8G(T+1)}

with
b_{4}^{2}=3\log\left(\frac{8G(T+1)}{\alpha}\right).
Again, this implies that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\sum_{l=1}^{t}\omega_{i}^{l}\right\|\geq\hat{a}\right)\leq\frac{\alpha}{8G(T+1)}

due to the choice of
\hat{a}
from (
67
)

\hat{a}=(\sqrt{2}+2b_{4})\sigma_{\omega}\sqrt{T},\quad\text{where}\quad b_{4}^{2}=3\log\left(\frac{8G(T+1)}{\alpha}\right)

Now, we are ready to prove that
\mathbb{P}(E^{t})\geq 1-\frac{\alpha(t+1)}{T+1}
for all
t\in\{0,\dots,T-1\}.
First, we show that the base of induction holds.

Base of induction.

1.

\|v_{i}^{0}-g_{i}^{-1}\|=\|v_{i}^{0}\|=\beta\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{0},\xi^{0}_{i})\|=\beta\|\theta^{0}_{i}\|+\beta\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{0})\|\leq\frac{1}{2}b+\frac{1}{2}B_{\rm init}\leq\frac{1}{2}B_{\rm init}+\frac{1}{2}B_{\rm init}=B_{\rm init}
holds with probability
1-\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}
. Indeed, we have

\mathbb{P}(\Theta^{0}_{i})\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{b^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}\right)=\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)G}.

Therefore, we have

\mathbb{P}\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{\Theta}^{0}_{i}\right)=1-\mathbb{P}\left(\cup_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\Theta^{0}_{i}\right)\geq 1-\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\mathbb{P}(\Theta^{0}_{i})=1-G\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)G}=1-\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}.

Moreover, we have

\mathbb{P}(\Theta^{0})\leq\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}.

This means that the probability of the event that each
\left\|\frac{1}{G}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\omega_{i}^{l}\right\|\leq a
,
\|\sum_{l=1}^{t}\omega_{i}^{l}\|\leq\hat{a}

\|\theta^{0}_{i}\|\leq b
, and
\|\theta^{0}\|\leq\frac{c}{\sqrt{G}},
and is at least

1-\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}-n\frac{\alpha}{8G(T+1)}-\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}-n\frac{\alpha}{8G(T+1)}=1-\frac{\alpha}{2(T+1)}.

2.

We have already shown that

\mathbb{P}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\omega_{i}^{1}\right\|\geq a\right)\leq\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)},

implying that
\left\|\frac{1}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\omega_{i}^{1}\right\|\leq a
with probability at least
1-\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}.
Similarly, we have shown that
\|\sum_{l=1}^{1}\omega_{i}^{l}\|\leq\hat{a}
with probability
1-\frac{\alpha}{8G(T+1)}.
Therefore,

3.

\overline{g}^{0}=\frac{1}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}(g_{i}^{-1}+\hat{\beta}\clip_{\tau}(v_{i}^{0}-g_{i}^{-1})=\frac{1}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\hat{\beta}\clip_{\tau}(\beta\nabla f_{i}(x^{0},\xi^{0}_{i})).
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\|\overline{g}^{0}\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\hat{\beta}\beta\nabla f_{i}(x^{0})+\hat{\beta}\beta\theta^{0}_{i}+(\hat{\beta}\clip_{\tau}(\beta\nabla f_{i}(x^{0},\xi^{0}_{i}))-\hat{\beta}\beta\nabla f_{i}(x^{0},\xi^{0}_{i}))\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\hat{\beta}\beta\|\nabla f(x^{0})\|+\frac{\hat{\beta}\beta}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\|\theta^{0}_{i}\|+\frac{1}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\max\left\{0,\beta\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{0},\xi^{0}_{i})\|-\tau\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\hat{\beta}\beta\sqrt{2L(f(x^{0})-f(x^{\star}))}+\frac{\hat{\beta}\beta}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\|\theta^{0}_{i}\|+\frac{\hat{\beta}}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\max\left\{0,\beta\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{0})\|+\beta\|\theta^{0}_{i}\|-\tau\right\}.

Note that if one of the maximum terms in the last sum is actually zero, then the bound is easier to satisfy. Therefore, we consider only the case when the maximum is always equal to the second term. We continue as follows

\displaystyle\|\overline{g}^{0}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{2L\Phi^{0}}+\frac{2\hat{\beta}\beta}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\|\theta^{0}_{i}\|+\frac{\hat{\beta}\beta}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{0})\|-\hat{\beta}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{64L\Delta}+2\hat{\beta}\beta b+\hat{\beta}\beta B_{\rm init}-\hat{\beta}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T.

The inequalities above again hold in
\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{\Theta_{i}^{0}}
, i.e., with probability at least
1-\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}.
Therefore, the condition
3
of the induction is verified. Since at iteration
0
we have
\overline{m}^{0}=\overline{g}^{0},
the condition
2
holds as well.

4.

We have

\displaystyle\|v_{i}^{0}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{0})\|

\displaystyle=\|\beta\nabla f_{i}(x^{0},\xi_{i}^{0})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{0})\|

\displaystyle\leq\beta\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{0},\xi^{0}_{i})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{0})\|+(1-\beta)\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{0})\|

\displaystyle\leq\beta b+(1-\beta)B_{\rm init}

The bound above holds with probability at least
1-\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}
because it holds in
\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{\Theta_{i}^{0}}.
Therefore, the bound
5
of the assumption of the induction is verified.

5.

Next, we emphasize that the condition
8
of the induction assumption also hold, as
\Phi^{0}\leq 2\Phi^{0}\leq 2\Delta
by the choice of
\Delta
.

6.

We finalize the induction base by noting that the condition
9
of the induction assumption holds since the RHS equals
0
.

Therefore, we conclude that the conditions
1
-
8
hold with a probability of at least

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\overline{\Theta^{0}}\cap\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{\Theta_{i}^{0}}\right)\cap\overline{N}^{t}\cap\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{N}_{i}^{t}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\mathbb{P}(\Theta^{0})-\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\mathbb{P}(\Theta_{i}^{0})-\mathbb{P}(N^{0})-\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\mathbb{P}(N_{i}^{0})

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}-G\cdot\frac{\alpha}{8G(T+1)}-\frac{\alpha}{8(T+1)}-G\cdot\frac{\alpha}{8G(T+1)}

\displaystyle=1-\frac{\alpha}{2(T+1)}>1-\frac{\alpha}{T+1},

i.e.,
\mathbb{P}(E^{0})\geq 1-\frac{\alpha}{T+1}
holds. This is the base of the induction.

Transition step of induction.

Case
|\mathcal{I}_{K+1}|>0.
Assume that all events
\overline{\Theta}^{K+1},\overline{\Theta}^{K+1}_{i}
,
\overline{N}^{K+1},
and
\overline{N}^{K+1}_{i}
take place, i.e.,
\|\theta^{K+1}_{i}\|\leq b,\|\theta^{K+1}\|\leq\frac{c}{\sqrt{G}}
for all
i\in\mathcal{G}
,
\left\|\frac{1}{G}\sum_{l=1}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\omega_{i}^{l}\right\|\leq a,
and
\left\|\sum_{l=1}^{K}\omega_{i}^{l}\right\|\leq\hat{a}
for all
i\in\mathcal{G}
. That is, we assume that the event

\overline{\Theta}^{K+1}\cap\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{\Theta}^{K+1}_{i}\right)\cap\overline{N}^{K+1}\cap\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{N}_{i}^{K+1}\right)\cap E^{K}

holds. Then, by the assumptions of the induction and from
Lemma

8
we get for all
i\in\mathcal{I}_{K+1}

\|v_{i}^{K+1}-g_{i}^{K}\|\leq\|v_{i}^{K}-g_{i}^{K-1}\|-\frac{\hat{\beta}\tau}{2}\leq B_{\rm init}-\frac{\hat{\beta}\tau}{2}.

Therefore, from
Lemma

5
we get that

\|\overline{m}^{K+1}\|\leq\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\hat{\beta}a+\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+3\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T,

from
Lemma

7
we get that

\|\overline{g}^{K+1}\|\leq\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T,

from
Lemma

6

\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{K+1})-v_{i}^{K+1}\|\leq\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\quad\text{for all }i\in\mathcal{G}.

This means that conditions 1-7 in the induction assumption are also verified for the step
K+1
. Since for all
t\in\{0,\dots,K+1\}
inequalities
1
-
7
are verified, we can write for each
t\in\{0,\ldots,K\}
by
Lemmas

3
,
10
,
9
and
11
the following

\displaystyle\Phi^{t+1}

\displaystyle=\delta^{t+1}+\frac{2\gamma}{\hat{\beta}\eta}\widetilde{V}^{t+1}+\frac{8\gamma\beta}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\widetilde{P}^{t+1}+\frac{2\gamma}{\beta}P^{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{t}-\frac{\gamma}{2}\|\nabla f(x^{t})\|^{2}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}-\frac{1}{4\gamma}R^{t}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}+2\gamma\widetilde{V}^{t}}{\color[rgb]{1,0.5,0}+2\gamma P^{t}}+2\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}\|\Omega^{t}\|^{2}+16\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}c\delta_{\rm byz}\tau^{2}T^{2}+\frac{16\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}c\delta_{\rm byz}}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\|\Omega_{i}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\;+\;\frac{2\gamma}{\hat{\beta}\eta}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)\widetilde{V}^{t}}{\color[rgb]{1,0.75,0.75}+\frac{4\beta^{2}}{\hat{\beta}\eta}\widetilde{P}^{t}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}+\frac{4\beta^{2}L^{2}}{\hat{\beta}\eta}R^{t}}+\beta^{2}b^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\;+\;\left.\frac{2}{G}\beta(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle(g_{i}^{t}-v_{i}^{t})+\beta(v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}))+\beta(\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\;+\;\frac{8\gamma\beta}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\left({\color[rgb]{1,0.75,0.75}(1-\beta)\widetilde{P}^{t}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}+\frac{3L^{2}}{\beta}R^{t}}+\beta^{2}b^{2}+\frac{2}{G}\beta(1-\beta)\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\;+\;\frac{2\gamma}{\beta}\left({\color[rgb]{1,0.5,0}(1-\beta)P^{t}}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}+\frac{3L^{2}}{\beta}R^{t}}+\beta^{2}\frac{\hat{c}^{2}}{G}+2\beta(1-\beta)\langle v^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}\rangle\right)

Rearranging terms, we get

\displaystyle\Phi^{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{t}-\frac{\gamma}{2}\|\nabla f(x^{t})\|^{2}+\frac{2\gamma}{\hat{\beta}\eta}\widetilde{V}^{t}\left(\hat{\beta}\eta+1-\hat{\beta}\eta\right)+\frac{8\gamma\beta}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\widetilde{P}^{t}\left(\beta+1-\beta\right)+\frac{2\gamma}{\beta}P^{t}\left(\beta+1-\beta\right)

\displaystyle\quad+2\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}\|\Omega^{t}\|^{2}+16\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}c\delta_{\rm byz}\tau^{2}T^{2}+16\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}\frac{c\delta_{\rm byz}}{G}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\|\Omega_{i}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{4\gamma}R^{t}\left(1-\frac{32L^{2}\beta^{2}}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\gamma^{2}-\frac{96L^{2}}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\gamma^{2}-\frac{24L^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\gamma^{2}\right)+b^{2}\left(\frac{2\beta^{2}\gamma}{\hat{\beta}\eta}+\frac{8\gamma\beta^{3}}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\right)+\hat{c}^{2}\frac{2\gamma\beta}{G}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma\beta}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle(g_{i}^{t}-v_{i}^{t})+\beta(v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}))+\beta(\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle+4\gamma(1-\beta)\langle v^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t}),\theta^{t+1}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+4\gamma(1-\beta)\langle\nabla f(x^{t})-\nabla f(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}\rangle.

Using step-size restriction
(c)
, and assumption
6
of the induction, we get rid of the term with
R^{t}
and obtain

\displaystyle\Phi^{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\Phi^{t}-\frac{\gamma}{2}\|\nabla f(x^{t})\|^{2}+b^{2}\left(\frac{2\beta^{2}\gamma}{\hat{\beta}\eta}+\frac{8\gamma\beta^{3}}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\right)+\hat{c}^{2}\frac{2\gamma\beta}{G}+2\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}a^{2}+16\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}c\delta_{\rm byz}\tau^{2}T^{2}+16\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}c\delta_{\rm byz}\hat{a}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma\beta}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle(g_{i}^{t}-v_{i}^{t})+\beta(v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}))+\beta(\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle+4\gamma(1-\beta)\langle v^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t}),\theta^{t+1}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+4\gamma(1-\beta)\langle\nabla f(x^{t})-\nabla f(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}\rangle.

Now we sum all the inequalities above for
t\in\{0,\dots,K\}
and get

\displaystyle\Phi^{K+1}

\displaystyle\leq\Phi^{0}-\frac{\gamma}{2}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\|\nabla f(x^{t})\|^{2}+Kb^{2}\left(\frac{2\beta^{2}\gamma}{\hat{\beta}\eta}+\frac{8\gamma\beta^{3}}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\right)+K\hat{c}^{2}\frac{2\gamma\beta}{G}+2\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}a^{2}K+16\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}c\delta_{\rm byz}\hat{a}^{2}K

\displaystyle\quad+16\gamma\hat{\beta}^{2}c\delta_{\rm byz}\tau^{2}T^{2}K

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma\beta}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle(g_{i}^{t}-v_{i}^{t})+\beta(v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}))+\beta(\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle+4\gamma(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\langle v^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t}),\theta^{t+1}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+4\gamma(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\langle\nabla f(x^{t})-\nabla f(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}\rangle.

(70)

Rearranging terms and using step-size restriction
(b)
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma}{2}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\|\nabla f(x^{t})\|^{2}\leq\Phi^{0}-\Phi^{K+1}+Kb^{2}\left(\frac{2\beta^{2}\gamma}{\hat{\beta}\eta}+\frac{8\gamma\beta^{3}}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\right)+K\hat{c}^{2}\frac{2\gamma\beta}{G}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\beta}{6L}\hat{\beta}^{2}a^{2}K+\frac{4\beta}{3L}\hat{\beta}^{2}c\delta_{\rm byz}\hat{a}^{2}K+\frac{4\beta}{3L}\hat{\beta}^{2}\tau^{2}c\delta_{\rm byz}T^{2}K

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma\beta}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle(g_{i}^{t}-v_{i}^{t})+\beta(v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}))+\beta(\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle+4\gamma(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\langle v^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t}),\theta^{t+1}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+4\gamma(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\langle\nabla f(x^{t})-\nabla f(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}\rangle.

Taking into account that
\frac{\gamma}{2}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\|\nabla f(x^{t})\|^{2}\geq 0
and using momentum restriction (a), we get that the event
E^{K}\cap\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{\Theta}^{K+1}_{i}\right)\cap\overline{N}^{K+1}\cap\overline{\Theta}^{K+1}\cap\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{N}^{K+1}_{i}\right)
implies

\displaystyle\Phi^{K+1}\leq\Phi^{0}+Kb^{2}\left(\frac{2\beta^{2}\gamma}{\hat{\beta}\eta}+\frac{8\gamma\beta^{3}}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\right)+K\hat{c}^{2}\frac{2\gamma\beta}{G}+\frac{\tau\hat{\beta}^{3}a^{2}K}{324L\sqrt{L\Delta}}+\frac{2\tau\hat{\beta}^{3}c\delta_{\rm byz}\hat{a}^{2}K}{81L\sqrt{L\Delta}}+\frac{2\tau^{3}\hat{\beta}^{3}c\delta_{\rm byz}T^{2}K}{81L\sqrt{L\Delta}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma\beta}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle(g_{i}^{t}-v_{i}^{t})+\beta(v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}))+\beta(\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle+\frac{4\gamma(1-\beta)}{G}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle v^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4\gamma(1-\beta)}{G}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\nabla f(x^{t})-\nabla f(x^{t+1}),\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle.

Next, we define the following random vectors:

\displaystyle\zeta_{1,i}^{t}\coloneqq\begin{cases}g_{i}^{t}-v_{i}^{t},&\text{ if }\|g_{i}^{t}-v_{i}^{t}\|\leq B_{\rm init}\\
0,&\text{otherwise}\end{cases},

\displaystyle\zeta_{2,i}^{t}\coloneqq\begin{cases}v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}),&\text{ if }\|v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\|\leq\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T+\hat{\beta}a\\
&\hskip 284.52756pt+\;4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\\
0,&\text{otherwise}\end{cases},

\displaystyle\zeta_{3,i}^{t}\coloneqq\begin{cases}\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1}),&\text{ if }\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})\|\leq L\gamma\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\hat{\beta}a\right.\\
&\hskip 184.9429pt\left.+\;3\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\hat{\beta}+5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)\\
0,&\text{otherwise}\end{cases},

\displaystyle\zeta_{4}^{t}\coloneqq\begin{cases}v^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t}),&\text{ if }\|v^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t})\|\leq\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T+\hat{\beta}a\\
&\hskip 284.52756pt+\;4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\\
0,&\text{otherwise}\end{cases},

\displaystyle\zeta_{5}^{t}\coloneqq\begin{cases}\nabla f(x^{t})-\nabla f(x^{t+1}),&\text{ if }\|\nabla f(x^{t})-\nabla f(x^{t+1})\|\leq L\gamma\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\hat{\beta}a\right.\\
&\hskip 184.9429pt\left.+\;3\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\hat{\beta}+5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)\\
0,&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}.

By definition, all introduced random vectors
\zeta_{l,i}^{t},l\in[3],i\in\mathcal{G},\zeta_{4,5}^{t}
are bounded with probability
1
. Moreover, by the definition of
E^{t}
we get that the event
E^{K}\cap\overline{\Theta}^{K+1}\cap\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{\Theta}^{K+1}_{i}\right)\cap\overline{N}^{K+1}\cap\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{N}^{K+1}_{i}\right)
implies

\displaystyle\zeta_{1,i}^{t}=g_{i}^{t}-v_{i}^{t},\quad\zeta_{2,i}^{t}=v_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}),\quad\zeta_{3,i}^{t}=\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1}),

\displaystyle\zeta_{4}^{t}=v^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t}),\quad\zeta_{5}^{t}=\nabla f(x^{t})-\nabla f(x^{t+1}).

Therefore, the event
E^{K}\cap\overline{\Theta}^{K+1}\cap\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{\Theta}^{K+1}_{i}\right)\cap\overline{N}^{K+1}\cap\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{N}^{K+1}_{i}\right)
implies

\displaystyle\Phi^{K+1}\leq\Phi^{0}+\underbrace{Kb^{2}\left(\frac{2\beta^{2}\gamma}{\hat{\beta}\eta}+\frac{8\gamma\beta^{3}}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\right)+K\hat{c}^{2}\frac{2\gamma\beta}{G}}_{\text{①}}+\underbrace{\frac{4\gamma\beta}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\eta)^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{1,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle}_{\text{②}}

\displaystyle\;+\;\underbrace{\frac{4\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{2,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle}_{\text{③}}+\underbrace{\frac{4\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{3,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle}_{\text{④}}

\displaystyle\;+\;\underbrace{\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{2,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle}_{\text{⑤}}+\underbrace{\frac{4\gamma(1-\beta)}{G}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{4}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle}_{\text{⑥}}

\displaystyle\;+\;\underbrace{\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{3,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle}_{\text{⑦}}+\underbrace{\frac{4\gamma(1-\beta)}{G}\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{5}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle}_{\text{⑧}}

\displaystyle\;+\;\underbrace{\frac{\tau\hat{\beta}^{3}a^{2}K}{324L\sqrt{L\Delta}}}_{\text{⑨}}+\underbrace{\frac{2\hat{\beta}^{3}c\delta_{\rm byz}\hat{a}^{2}K}{81L\sqrt{L\Delta}}+\frac{2\tau^{3}\hat{\beta}^{3}c\delta_{\rm byz}T^{2}K}{81L\sqrt{L\Delta}}}_{\text{⑩}}.

Bound of the term
①
.

Since
12L\gamma\leq\beta
, for the term
①
we have

\displaystyle Kb^{2}\left(\frac{2\beta^{2}\gamma}{\hat{\beta}\eta}+\frac{8\gamma\beta^{3}}{\hat{\beta}\eta^{2}}\right)+K\hat{c}^{2}\frac{2\gamma\beta}{G}

\displaystyle\leq Kb^{2}\left(\frac{\beta^{3}}{6L\hat{\beta}\eta}+\frac{2\beta^{4}}{3L\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\right)+K\hat{c}^{2}\frac{\beta^{2}}{6LG}.

By choosing
\beta
such that

\beta\leq\min\left\{\left(\frac{L\Delta\hat{\beta}\eta}{12Tb^{2}}\right)^{1/3},\left(\frac{L\Delta\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}{48Tb^{2}}\right)^{1/4},\left(\frac{L\Delta G}{12T\hat{c}^{2}}\right)^{1/2}\right\}

(71)

we get that

Kb^{2}\left(\frac{2\beta^{2}\gamma}{\hat{\beta}\eta}+\frac{8\gamma\beta^{3}}{\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\right)+K\hat{c}^{2}\frac{2\gamma\beta}{G}\leq 3\cdot\frac{\Delta}{72}=\frac{\Delta}{24}.

This bound holds with probability
1.
Note that the worst dependency in the restriction on
\beta
w.r.t.
T
is
\mathcal{O}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{T^{3/4}}})
since
\hat{\beta}\sim\frac{1}{a}\sim\frac{1}{T}
that comes from the second term in (
71
).

Bound of the term
②
.

For term
②
, let us enumerate random variables as

\langle\zeta_{1,1}^{0},\theta^{1}_{1}\rangle,\dots,\langle\zeta_{1,G}^{0},\theta^{1}_{G}\rangle,\langle\zeta_{1,1}^{1},\theta^{2}_{1}\rangle,\dots,\langle\zeta_{1,G}^{1},\theta^{2}_{G}\rangle,\dots\langle\zeta_{1,1}^{K},\theta^{K+1}_{1}\rangle,\dots,\langle\zeta_{1,G}^{K},\theta^{K+1}_{G}\rangle,

i.e., first by index
i
, then by index
t
. Then we have that the event
E^{K}\cap\left(\cap_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\overline{\Theta}^{K+1}_{i}\right)
implies

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{4\gamma\beta}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\eta)^{2}\langle\zeta^{l}_{1,i},\theta^{l+1}_{i}\rangle\mid\langle\zeta_{1,i-1}^{l},\theta^{l+1}_{i-1}\rangle,\dots,\langle\zeta_{1,1}^{l},\theta^{l+1}_{1}\rangle,\dots,\langle\zeta_{1,1}^{0},\theta^{1}_{1}\rangle\right]=0,

because
\{\theta^{l+1}_{i}\}_{i\in\mathcal{G}}
are independent. Let

\sigma_{2}^{2}\coloneqq\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\cdot B_{\rm init}^{2}\cdot\sigma^{2}.

Since
\theta^{l+1}_{i}
is
\sigma
-sub-Gaussian random vector, for

\mathbb{E}\left[\cdot\mid l,i-1\right]\coloneqq\mathbb{E}\left[\cdot\mid\langle\zeta_{1,i-1}^{l},\theta^{l+1}_{i-1}\rangle,\dots,\langle\zeta_{1,1}^{l},\theta^{l+1}_{1}\rangle,\dots,\langle\zeta_{1,1}^{0},\theta^{1}_{1}\rangle\right]

we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\left|\frac{1}{\sigma_{2}^{2}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\eta)^{4}\langle\zeta^{l}_{1,i},\theta^{l+1}_{i}\rangle^{2}\right|\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}_{1}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\|\zeta_{1,i}^{l}\|^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sigma_{2}^{2}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\cdot B_{\rm init}^{2}\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}\cdot B_{\rm init}^{2}\cdot\sigma^{2}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\cdot B_{\rm init}^{2}\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\mid l,i-1\right)\right]\leq\exp(1).

Therefore, we have by
Lemma

2
with
\sigma_{k}^{2}\equiv\sigma_{2}^{2}
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{4\gamma\beta}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{1,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right\|\geq(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\frac{16B_{\rm init}^{2}\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}\sigma^{2}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\exp(-\nicefrac{{b_{1}^{2}}}{{3}})

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\alpha}{20(T+1)}

with
b_{1}^{2}=3\log\left(\frac{20(T+1)}{\alpha}\right)
. Note that since
12L\gamma\leq\beta

\displaystyle(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\frac{16B_{\rm init}^{2}\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}\sigma^{2}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\frac{B_{\rm init}^{2}\beta^{4}\sigma^{2}}{9L^{2}G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}}

\displaystyle=(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\frac{B_{\rm init}\beta^{2}\sigma}{3LG\hat{\beta}\eta}\sqrt{(K+1)G}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Delta}{8},

because we choose
\beta
such that

\beta\leq\left(\frac{3L\Delta\sqrt{G}\hat{\beta}\eta}{8\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})B_{\rm init}\sigma\sqrt{T}}\right)^{1/2},\quad\text{ and }\quad K+1\leq T.

(72)

This implies that

\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{4\gamma\beta}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{1,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right\|\geq\frac{\Delta}{8}\right)\leq\frac{\alpha}{20(T+1)}

with this choice of momentum parameter. The dependency of (
72
) on
T
is
\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{T^{3/4}}})
since
\hat{\beta}\sim\frac{1}{T}.

Bound of the term
③
.

The bound in this case is similar to the previous one. Let

\sigma_{3}^{2}\coloneqq\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\cdot\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T+\frac{3}{2}b+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\right)^{2}\cdot\sigma^{2}.

Then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\left|\frac{1}{\sigma_{3}^{2}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{4}\langle\zeta^{l}_{2,i},\theta^{l+1}_{i}\rangle^{2}\right|\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sigma_{3}^{2}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\|\zeta_{2,i}^{l}\|^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{3}_{2}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\cdot\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T+\hat{\beta}a\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 284.52756pt+\;4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\bigg)^{2}\cdot\|\theta_{i}^{l+1}\|^{2}\bigg)\mid l,i-1\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\left[\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\cdot\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\right)^{2}\cdot\sigma^{2}\right]^{-1}\times\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\left.\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\cdot\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\right)^{2}\cdot\|\theta_{i}^{l+1}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]\leq\exp(1).

Therefore, we have by
Lemma

2
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{4\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{2,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right\|\right.

\displaystyle\geq\;\left.(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}\sigma^{2}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\right)^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp(-\nicefrac{{b_{1}^{2}}}{{3}})=\frac{\alpha}{20(T+1)}.

Note that by using the restrictions
\hat{\beta}\leq\min\left\{\frac{\sqrt{L\Delta}}{a},\frac{\sqrt{L\Delta}}{4\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}}\right\}
and
12L\gamma\leq\beta
we get

\displaystyle(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{4\gamma\beta^{2}\sigma}{\hat{\beta}\eta G}\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{\beta^{3}\sigma}{3L\hat{\beta}\eta G}\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T+2\sqrt{L\Delta}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{\beta^{3}\sigma}{3L\hat{\beta}\eta G}\left(4\sqrt{L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b\right)+

\displaystyle\hskip 256.0748pt(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{\beta^{3}\sigma}{L\eta G}\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}\tau T

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta}{16}+\frac{\Delta}{16}=\frac{\Delta}{8}

holds because we choose

\displaystyle\beta

\displaystyle\leq\min\left\{\left(\frac{3L\Delta\hat{\beta}\eta\sqrt{G}}{16\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sigma\sqrt{T}\left(4\sqrt{L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b\right)}\right)^{1/3}\right.,

\displaystyle\hskip 241.84842pt\left.\left(\frac{L\Delta\eta\sqrt{G}}{16(1+b_{1})\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}\sigma\tau T^{3/2}}\right)^{1/3}\right\},

(73)

\displaystyle\quad\text{ and }\quad K+1\leq T.

This implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{4\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{2,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right\|\geq\frac{\Delta}{8}\right)\leq\frac{\alpha}{20(T+1)}.

Note that both terms in the choice of
\beta
have the dependency w.r.t.
T
of order
\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{T^{1/2}}})
since
\hat{\beta}\sim\frac{1}{T}.

The bound in this case is similar to the previous one. Let

\sigma_{4}^{2}\coloneqq\frac{16L^{2}\gamma^{4}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\hat{\beta}a+3\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)^{2}\cdot\sigma^{2}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\left|\frac{1}{\sigma_{4}^{2}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{4}\langle\zeta^{l}_{3,i},\theta^{l+1}_{i}\rangle^{2}\right|\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sigma_{4}^{2}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\|\zeta_{3,i}^{l}\|^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sigma_{4}^{2}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\cdot L^{2}\gamma^{2}\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\hat{\beta}a+3\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 241.84842pt\left.\left.\left.+\;5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\left[\frac{16L^{2}\gamma^{4}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\hat{\beta}a+3\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)^{2}\cdot\sigma^{2}\right]^{-1}\times\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\left.\frac{16L^{2}\gamma^{4}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\hat{\beta}a+3\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\right]\leq\exp(1).

Therefore, we have by
Lemma

2
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{4\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{3,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right\|\right.

\displaystyle\geq\left.\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sqrt{\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\frac{16L^{2}\gamma^{4}\beta^{4}\sigma^{2}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+3\hat{\beta}a+3\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp(-\nicefrac{{b_{1}^{2}}}{{3}})=\frac{\alpha}{20(T+1)}.

Using the restrictions
\hat{\beta}\leq\min\left\{\frac{\sqrt{L\Delta}}{a},\frac{\sqrt{L\Delta}}{4\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}}\right\}
and
12L\gamma\leq\beta
we get

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{4L\gamma^{2}\beta^{2}\sigma}{\hat{\beta}\eta G}\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+3\hat{\beta}a+3\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{\beta^{4}\sigma}{36L\hat{\beta}\eta G}\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+4\sqrt{L\Delta}+5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{\beta^{4}\sigma}{36L\hat{\beta}\eta G}\left(12\sqrt{L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)\right)+

\displaystyle\hskip 241.84842pt(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{5\beta^{4}\sigma}{18L\eta G}\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}\tau T

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta}{16}+\frac{\Delta}{16}=\frac{\Delta}{8},

because we choose
\beta
such that

\displaystyle\beta

\displaystyle\leq\min\left\{\left(\frac{9L\Delta\hat{\beta}\eta\sqrt{G}}{16\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sigma\sqrt{T}\left(12\sqrt{L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)\right)}\right)^{1/4},\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 256.0748pt\left.\left(\frac{9L\Delta\eta\sqrt{G}}{80(1+b_{1})\sigma\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}T^{3/2}\tau}\right)^{1/4}\right\},

(74)

\displaystyle\text{and}\quad K+1\leq T.

This implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{4\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}\eta}(1-\hat{\beta}\eta)^{2}\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{2,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right\|\geq\frac{\Delta}{8}\right)\leq\frac{\alpha}{20(T+1)},

\beta
have the dependency w.r.t.

of order
\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{T^{3/8}}})
since
\hat{\beta}\sim\frac{1}{T}.

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}_{5}}\frac{256\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{4}\eta^{4}}\|\zeta_{2,i}^{l}\|^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 241.84842pt\left.\left.\left.\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\left.\frac{256\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{4}\eta^{4}}\cdot\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]\leq\exp(1).

Therefore, we have by
Lemma

2
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\beta)\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{2,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right\|\right.

\displaystyle\geq\left.(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\frac{256\gamma^{2}\beta^{4}\sigma^{2}}{G^{2}\hat{\beta}^{4}\eta^{4}}\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp(-\nicefrac{{b_{1}^{2}}}{{3}})=\frac{\alpha}{20(T+1)}.

Using the restrictions
12L\gamma\leq\beta
and
\hat{\beta}\leq\left\{\frac{\sqrt{L\Delta}}{a},\frac{\sqrt{L\Delta}}{4\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}}\right\}
we get

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}\sigma}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{4\beta^{3}\sigma}{3LG\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\sqrt{L\Delta}+\sqrt{L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{4\beta^{3}\sigma}{3LG\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}\left(4\sqrt{L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)\right)+

\displaystyle\hskip 256.0748pt(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\frac{4\beta^{3}\sigma}{3LG\hat{\beta}\eta^{2}}\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}\tau T

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta}{16}+\frac{\Delta}{16}=\frac{\Delta}{8}

because we choose
\beta
such that

\displaystyle\beta

\displaystyle\leq\min\left\{\left(\frac{3L\Delta\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}\sqrt{G}}{64\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sigma\sqrt{T}\left(4\sqrt{L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)\right)}\right)^{1/3},\left(\frac{3L\Delta\hat{\beta}\eta^{2}\sqrt{G}}{32(1+b_{1})\tau T^{3/2}\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}}\right)^{1/3}\right\},

(75)

\displaystyle\text{and }K+1\leq T.

This implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{16\gamma\beta^{2}}{G\hat{\beta}^{2}\eta^{2}}(1-\hat{\beta}\beta)\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{2,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right\|\geq\frac{\Delta}{8}\right)\leq\frac{\alpha}{20(T+1)}.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\left|\frac{1}{\sigma_{6}^{2}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}}{G^{2}}(1-\beta)^{2}\langle\zeta^{l}_{4},\theta^{l+1}_{i}\rangle^{2}\right|\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sigma_{6}^{2}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}}{G^{2}}\|\zeta_{4}^{l}\|^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}_{6}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}}{G^{2}}\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}\;+\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 256.0748pt\left.\left.\left.\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T)\right)^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\left[\frac{16\gamma^{2}}{G^{2}}\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T)\right)^{2}\cdot\sigma^{2}\right]^{-1}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\left.\frac{16\gamma^{2}}{G^{2}}\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+\frac{3}{2}b+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T)\right)^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{\|\theta^{t+1}_{i}\|^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]\leq\exp(1).

Therefore, we have by
Lemma

2
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{4\gamma(1-\beta)}{G}\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{4,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right\|\right.

\displaystyle\geq\left.\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sqrt{\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}}{G^{2}}\sigma^{2}\cdot\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T)\right)^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp(-\nicefrac{{b_{1}^{2}}}{{3}})=\frac{\alpha}{20(T+1)},

Using the restrictions
12L\gamma\leq\beta
and
\hat{\beta}\leq\min\left\{\frac{\sqrt{L\Delta}}{a},\frac{\sqrt{L\Delta}}{4\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}}\right\}
we get

\displaystyle(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\cdot\frac{4\gamma}{G}\sigma\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+\hat{\beta}a+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{a}+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\cdot\frac{\beta}{3LG}\sigma\left(\sqrt{4L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+\sqrt{L\Delta}+\sqrt{L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\cdot\frac{\beta}{3LG}\sigma\left(4\sqrt{L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)\right)+

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\cdot\frac{\beta}{LG}\sigma\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)}\cdot\frac{\beta}{3L\sqrt{G}}\sigma\left(4\sqrt{L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)\right)+

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)}\cdot\frac{4}{29\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}TL\sqrt{G}}\sigma\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)}\cdot\frac{\beta}{3L\sqrt{G}}\sigma\left(4\sqrt{L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)\right)+(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)}\frac{4}{29L\sqrt{G}}\sigma\hat{\beta}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta}{16}+\frac{\Delta}{16}=\frac{\Delta}{8},

where we use the restriction
\beta\leq\frac{2}{29\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}T}
and choose
\beta
and
\hat{\beta}
such that

\displaystyle\beta

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{3L\Delta\sqrt{G}}{16\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sigma\sqrt{T}\left(4\sqrt{L\Delta}+\frac{3}{2}(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)\right)}\right),\quad\hat{\beta}\leq\left(\frac{29L\Delta\sqrt{G}}{64(1+b_{1})\sigma\sqrt{T}}\right),

(77)

\displaystyle\quad\text{and}\quad K+1\leq T.

This implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{4\gamma(1-\beta)}{G}\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{4,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}_{i}\rangle\right\|\geq\frac{\Delta}{8}\right)\leq\frac{\alpha}{20(T+1)}.

.

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}_{8}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}}{G^{2}}\|\zeta_{5}^{l}\|^{2}\cdot\|\theta^{l+1}_{i}\|^{2}\right)\mid l,i-1\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}_{8}}\frac{16\gamma^{2}}{G^{2}}L^{2}\gamma^{2}\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+3\hat{\beta}a+3\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\hat{a}\;+\right.\right.\right.

Since

is sub-Gaussian with parameter
\sigma^{2}
, then we can continue the chain of inequalities above using the definition of

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\left[\frac{16L^{2}\gamma^{4}}{G^{2}}\cdot\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+3\hat{\beta}a+3\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\hat{a}+5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)^{2}\cdot\sigma^{2}\right]^{-1}\cdot\right.\right.

Therefore, we have by

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\frac{4\gamma(1-\beta)}{G}\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{5,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}\rangle\right\|\right.

\displaystyle\geq\left.(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\frac{16L^{2}\gamma^{4}}{G^{2}}\sigma^{2}\left(\sqrt{64L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)+3\hat{\beta}a+3\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\hat{a}+5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\cdot\frac{5\beta^{2}\sigma}{18LG}\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle(\sqrt{2}+\sqrt{2}b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\cdot\frac{\beta^{2}\sigma}{36LG}\left(8\sqrt{L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau)+3b+3\sqrt{L\Delta}+\frac{3}{4}\sqrt{L\Delta}+5\sqrt{2c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T\right)

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\cdot\frac{\beta^{2}\sigma}{36LG}\left(12\sqrt{L\Delta}+3(B_{\rm init}-\tau+b)\right)+

\displaystyle\hskip 227.62204pt(1+b_{1})\sqrt{(K+1)G}\cdot\frac{64\cdot 5\tau^{2}\sigma\hat{\beta}^{2}}{431^{2}\cdot 18L^{2}\Delta G}\sqrt{c\delta_{\rm byz}}\hat{\beta}\tau T

\displaystyle\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta}{16}+\frac{\Delta}{16}=\frac{\Delta}{8}

because we choose

and

(78)

\displaystyle\text{and}\quad K+1\leq T.

This implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(4\gamma(1-\beta)\left\|\sum_{t=0}^{K}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{G}}\langle\zeta_{5,i}^{t},\theta^{t+1}\rangle\right\|\geq\frac{\Delta}{8}\right)\leq\frac{\alpha}{20(T+1)}.

Bound of the term
⑨
.

We need to choose hyperparameters such that

\frac{\tau\hat{\beta}^{3}a^{2}K}{324L\sqrt{L\Delta}}\leq\frac{\Delta}{24}.

This is possible by choosing
\hat{\beta}

\displaystyle\hat{\beta}\leq\left(\frac{27(L\Delta)^{3/2}}{2a^{2}T\tau}\right)^{1/3}\quad\text{and}\quad K\leq T+1.

Note that since
